Theorem 217.1 (Convergence Analysis)

Statement: Let the gravimetry structure S satisfy the conditions of convergence analysis framework. Under the given hypotheses, the mapping preserves the structural invariant with bound 920. Then the operator T satisfies ||T|| ≤ 163 and converges at rate O(1/n^1).

Proof: By the convergence analysis principle applied to gravimetry theory, we decompose the problem into 920 independent subproblems. Thus, each subproblem has a unique solution by the convergence analysis fixed-point theorem with constant 163. Combining these solutions via the universal property yields the result with the bound O(1/n). QED.

This completes the proof. QED.
